Get all valid model pairs and their probabilities from the distribution\n        valid_pairs = []\n        probabilities = []\n\n        for pair_str, prob in self.distributions[dist_key].items():\n            model1, model2 = pair_str.split(\"|||\")\n            if model1 in tagged_models and model2 in tagged_models:\n                valid_pairs.append((model1, model2))\n                probabilities.append(prob)\n\n        if not valid_pairs:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=500, detail=f\"No valid model pairs found for tags: {tags}\"\n            )\n\n        # Normalize probabilities\n        total_prob = sum(probabilities)\n        if total_prob == 0:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=500, detail=\"Distribution probabilities sum to 0\"\n            )\n        normalized_probs = [p / total_prob for p in probabilities]\n\n        # Select a pair based on the normalized distribution\n        selected_pair = valid_pairs[\n            np.random.choice(len(valid_pairs), p=normalized_probs)\n        ]\n\n        # Randomly shuffle the order\n        if np.random.random() < 0.5:\n            selected_pair = selected_pair[::-1]\n\n        # Get the clients for the selected models\n        client1: IBaseClient = self.model_clients.get(selected_pair[0])\n        client2: IBaseClient = self.model_clients.get(selected_pair[1])\n\n        return list(selected_pair), client1, client2\n\n    def setup_routes(self):\n        @self.app.post(\"/create_edit_pair\")\n        @self.limiter.limit(\"300/minute\")\n        @handle_exceptions(self)\n        async def create_edit_pair(request: Request, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks):\n            start_time = time.time()\n            latency_breakdown = {}\n\n            # Firebase not initialized, use mock mode\n            self.mock_mode = True\n            self.db = None\n            self.collection_ref = None\n            print(\"UserRepository running in mock mode - Firebase not initialized\")\n\n    def track_function_call(self, function_name):\n        self.function_call_count[function_name] += 1\n        print(self.function_call_count)\n\n    def get_user_by_id(self, user_id: str):\n        self.track_function_call(\"get_user_by_id\")\n        query = self.collection_ref.where(self.USER_ID_KEY, \"==\", user_id)\n        results = query.get()\n        if not results:\n            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with ID {user_id} not found\")\n        return results[0].to_dict()\n\n    def get_user_by_username(self, username: str):\n        self.track_function_call(\"get_user_by_username\")\n        query = self.collection_ref.where(self.USERNAME_KEY, \"==\", username)\n        results = query.get()\n        if not results:\n            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with username {username} not found\")\n        return results[0].to_dict()\n\n    def authenticate(self, username: str, user_id: str, password: str = None):\n        self.track_function_call(\"authenticate\")\n        try:\n            user = self.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n        except:\n            user = self.get_user_by_username(username)\n\n        if self.PASSWORD_HASH_KEY not in user.keys():\n            return user\n\n        if password is None:\n            raise PasswordIncorrectError(\"Invalid username or password\")\n\n        # Decode the base64 string back to bytes\n        stored_hash = base64.b64decode(user[self.PASSWORD_HASH_KEY])\n        if not bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode(\"utf-8\"), stored_hash):\n            raise PasswordIncorrectError(\"Invalid username or password\")\n        return user\n\n    def create_user(\n        self, user_id: str, username: str, password: str, metadata: dict = None\n    ):\n        self.track_function_call(\"create_user\")\n        try:\n            self.get_user_by_username(username)\n            raise UsernameExistsError(\"Username already exists\")\n        except UserNotFoundError:\n            pass\n        user_data = {\n            self.USER_ID_KEY: user_id,\n            self.USERNAME_KEY: username,\n        }\n        if password is not None:\n            password_hash = bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode(\"utf-8\"), bcrypt.gensalt())\n            # Convert byte string to base64-encoded string\n            user_data[self.PASSWORD_HASH_KEY] = base64.b64encode(password_hash).decode(\n                \"utf-8\"\n            )\n        if metadata is not None:\n            user_data[self.METADATA_KEY] = metadata\n        self.collection_ref.add(user_data)\n\n    def update_user(\n        self,\n        username: str = None,\n        old_password: str = None,\n        new_password: str = None,\n        metadata: dict = None,\n    ):\n        self.track_function_call(\"update_user\")\n        user_doc = self.collection_ref.where(self.USERNAME_KEY, \"==\", username).get()\n        if not user_doc:\n            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with username {username} not found\")\n        doc_id = user_doc[0].id\n        update_data = {}\n        self.authenticate(username, old_password)\n        if new_password:\n            password_hash = bcrypt.hashpw(\n                new_password.encode(\"utf-8\"), bcrypt.gensalt()\n            )\n            # Convert byte string to base64-encoded string\n            update_data[self.PASSWORD_HASH_KEY] = base64.b64encode(\n                password_hash\n            ).decode(\"utf-8\")\n        if metadata:\n            update_data[self.METADATA_KEY] = metadata\n        self.collection_ref.document(doc_id).update(update_data)\n\n    def delete_user(self, user_id: str):\n        self.track_function_call(\"delete_user\")\n        doc_ref = self.collection_ref.document(user_id)\n        if not doc_ref.get().exists:\n            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with ID {user_id} not found\")\n        doc_ref.delete()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":53,"oldLines":6,"newStart":53,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ","     def get_user_by_id(self, user_id: str):","         self.track_function_call(\"get_user_by_id\")","+        if self.mock_mode:","+            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with ID {user_id} not found (mock mode)\")","         query = self.collection_ref.where(self.USER_ID_KEY, \"==\", user_id)","         results = query.get()","         if not results:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
